### 2026-09-24 14:37:53  #MKT News #Important #Market Themes #AI Revolution #CSI300 #Impact bullish #SSE Composite

【Shanghai Financial Regulatory Bureau issues measures to promote AI adoption in banking and insurance】Shanghai Financial Regulatory Bureau published measures encouraging banks and insurers to deploy artificial intelligence to optimize intelligent marketing and omnichannel service, raising service efficiency, precision and customer experience. The measures push intelligent redesign of business processes and deeper AI use in lending, claims adjudication and operations to boost efficiency and risk management. They mandate strengthened internal-management automation across office functions, investment research, R&D and operations, and require building intelligent risk-control systems with multidimensional models to improve forward-looking monitoring and prevention of media/public sentiment risks, illegal fundraising, fraud and money laundering. The regulator supports cautious exploration of next‑generation intelligent terminals and financial intelligent agents provided risks remain controllable, aiming to shift services from digital enablement to intelligence-driven delivery.

【中郵證券：維持長高電氣“買入”評級，看好GIS新產品的持續開拓】中郵證券研報指出，長高電氣訂單穩健，看好GIS新產品的持續開拓。2026H1公司歸母淨利潤5.8億元，同比+451.4%（微超預告上線），其中2026Q2公司歸母淨利潤5.3億，同環比分別+628.7%/+827.5%。電網中標穩健增長，新產品持續開發。“十五五”電網有望適度超前建設。“十五五”期間，我國將新增投產15回左右特高壓直流輸電通道，西電東送規模超過4.2億千瓦，建成6個以上區域電網互濟工程，提升電力互濟能力約4000萬千瓦，“十五五”全國電網固定資產投資達到5萬億元以上，較“十四五”同期相比增長超80%。公司有望充分受益電網投資的景氣度。該行上調盈利預測，預測公司2026-2028年營收分別為21.0/26.3/32.1億元，歸母淨利潤分別為6.9/4.7/5.9億元，對應PE分別為11/16/13倍，維持“買入”評級。

【國家郵政局：“兩節”期間寄遞服務需求旺盛，各企業要科學研判假期業務量峰值和流向流量】近日，國家郵政局印發通知，部署2026年中秋和國慶假期郵政快遞業安全生產和服務保障工作。通知指出，“兩節”期間人流、物流、車流高度集中，寄遞服務需求旺盛，各類安全風險交織疊加。各級郵政管理部門和各企業要深刻汲取近期全國重大生產安全事故教訓，嚴格落實部門監管責任和企業主體責任，加強風險研判，做到早發現、早預警、早處置，牢牢守住安全底線紅線。通知要求，各企業要科學研判假期業務量峰值和流向流量，精準儲備人力、物力、運力，確保郵件快件收投有序、運遞暢通；切實保障重點物資和民生物資寄遞服務，嚴格履行服務承諾，及時妥善處理延誤、丟失損毀、賠償等服務質量異議；郵政集團要確保郵政普遍服務持續提供。通知要求，各地各企業要完善矛盾糾紛排查化解機制，健全快遞員訴求直達機制，維護快遞員群體合法權益。

UK Natgas Prices Advance on Middle East Uncertainty — UK natural gas prices rose to 186 pence per therm Thursday, recovering from a three-week low amid uncertainty over a diplomatic resolution to the Middle East conflict that could reopen the Strait of Hormuz. Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian said that Tehran would never surrender to the US but remained open to dialogue and diplomacy, while setting conditions for a return to negotiations. The key waterway remains largely closed, impeding LNG flows from the Persian Gulf. Europe’s gas storage position remains a key concern as the winter heating season approaches. Storage facilities are around 70% full, still below the five-year seasonal average and the region’s 80% target. Goldman Sachs expects European and Asian gas prices to rise sharply if Gulf LNG exports remain constrained.

【DeepSeek完成75億美元融資年化營收突破10億美元】據兩名知情人士透露，DeepSeek當前年化營收執行率已達10億美元。短短數月前，該公司這一指標尚不足5億美元。營收增長一方面得益於近期上調API定價，另一方面也源於其大模型持續受到市場熱捧。DeepSeek CEO梁文鋒在近期投資者會議上披露了上述最新營收資料。公司正推進第二輪融資，並籌備在上交所上市，這一營收表現有望提振投資者信心。DeepSeek計劃在10月底前完成本輪融資，目標募資500億元人民幣（約合75億美元），估值目標5000億元人民幣。知情人士稱，DeepSeek營收增長部分來自上月的調價動作：模型呼叫價格上調至原來的2.3倍至4.5倍。梁文鋒在會上表示，調價並未造成客戶流失，使用者需求依舊強勁。知情人士轉述，梁文鋒向投資者透露，DeepSeek持續將大部分資源投入新模型研發。公司把70%以上算力分配給模型訓練，留給已有模型推理執行的算力不足30%。梁文鋒還向投資者表示，內部測試顯示，公司輕量化模型可在遊戲顯示卡上穩定執行，能夠處理使用者絕大多數日常任務。

Norway Loan Debt Rises 4.5% in August — Norway’s general public domestic loan debt rose by 4.5% year-on-year to NOK 8.05 trillion at the end of August 2026, following an eight-month low of 4.3% growth in the previous month. Lending growth to non-financial corporations increased to 4.8% from 4.0%, reaching NOK 2.42 trillion, while household loan growth remained steady at 4.6%, totaling NOK 4.79 trillion. In contrast, municipal government loan debt growth eased to 3.0% from 3.4% a month earlier, reaching NOK 8.38 billion. On a seasonally adjusted annualized one-month basis, total loan debt for the general public grew by 5.3%, following a 3.8% increase in the prior period.

### 2026-09-24 14:06:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Saudi Arabia #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Saudi Arabia Trade Surplus Smallest in 6 Months — Saudi Arabia’s trade surplus narrowed to SAR 14.4 billion in July 2026 from SAR 19.1 billion in the same month last year, marking the smallest trade surplus since January as exports dropped more than imports. Exports fell 17.2% year-on-year to an over five-year low of SAR 84.4 billion, mainly driven by a 12.8% fall in oil exports, which represented 71.0% of total exports. Meanwhile, non-oil exports shed 14.8% amid a decline in shipments of plastic, rubber, and their articles (-17.8%), which accounted for 29.8% of total non-oil exports. China was the leading destination for Saudi exports, accounting for 13.4% of the total, followed by the UAE (10.0%) and Japan (8.7%). Imports fell 15.4% to an over two-year low of SAR 70.4 billion, largely due to a sharp decline in machinery, electrical equipment, and parts thereof (-26.6%). China remained the largest source of imports, making up 22.7% of the total, followed by the US (8.4%) and Switzerland (6.9%).

【《穴位貼敷用藥規範》等四項針灸領域國家標準下月起實施】為進一步助力針灸技術推廣，推動中醫藥傳承創新發展，由國家中醫藥局提出並歸口、市場監管總局批准釋出的《穴位貼敷用藥規範》等四項針灸領域推薦性國家標準自10月1日起實施。《穴位貼敷用藥規範》規範穴位貼敷的藥物使用，強化用藥安全，細化技術操作，增加穴位覆蓋面積規定，科學界定適應證，為基層臨床提供可直接參照的技術規範。《針灸異常情況處理》明確暈針、滯針、針刺性氣胸、燙傷等11類常見異常情況的科學處理方法，強化針灸診療全流程風險防控。《針灸技術操作規範 第4部分：三稜針》系統規範了三稜針療法的概念定義、操作步驟、禁忌事項等核心內容，並對點刺法、刺絡法、散刺法、挑治法四種技術操作建立統一標準。《針灸技術操作規範 第6部分：穴位注射》則規範了穴位注射療法的相關概念、操作步驟與要求、注意事項、禁忌等，為臨床、教學及科研領域提供普適性準則。

【瑞銀：iPhone 18平均等候時間撇除中國後同比大致持平，維持蘋果“中性”評級】瑞銀髮表研報指，追蹤逾30個地區蘋果iPhone供應情況的資料顯示，Pro及Pro Max平均等候時間按周分別上升約3日及約2日。兩款型號平均同比差距約為少1日，較上週初值少2日有所改善。撇除中國同比少4日的差距後，各地區等候時間大致持平，顯示需求趨勢邊際或有改善。iPhone 18 Pro Max平均等候時間約24日，對比17 Pro Max約25日及16 Pro Max約23日；iPhone 18 Pro約17日，對比17 Pro約18日及16 Pro約17日。該行認為，進入10月Duo釋出前的等候時間是重要觀察資料，而中國iPhone供應情況值得關注，因先前調查顯示當地對摺疊機、尤其是蘋果摺疊機興趣較其他市場為高。該行維持iPhone預測不變，估計2026年9月季度iPhone收入559億美元，同比升14%，出貨5500萬部，同比升2.8%；2026財年iPhone收入2524億美元，同比升20.4%，出貨2.61億部，同比升15.4%。該行維持蘋果“中性”評級及目標價296美元，相當於約29倍該行混合預測2027/2028歷年每股盈利預測10.31美元。

### 2026-09-24 13:22:08  #Trading Economics #Economy #Finland #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Finland Producer Inflation Hits 3-Month High — Producer prices in Finland rose 7.3% year-on-year in August 2026, accelerating from 6.9% in July and the highest recorded since May 2026. The acceleration was mainly driven by faster price growth in manufacturing (8.2% vs 7.6% in July) particularly refined petroleum products and basic metals. Moreover, prices for computer, electronic and optical products (19.3%), basic metals (13.4%) and rubber and plastic products (9.2%) also contributed. Producer price growth in mining and quarrying also accelerated (12.1% vs 11.9%). Meanwhile, prices in electricity, gas, steam and air-conditioning supply fell further (-5.4% vs -4.2%), while growth in water supply, sewerage, waste management and remediation services eased (10.9% vs 11.5%). On a monthly basis, producer prices increased 1.2% from a 0.1% rise in the previous month.

Rupiah Under strain as Dollar Rises, Fiscal Concerns Mount — The Indonesian rupiah slipped to near IDR 17,900 per U.S. dollar on Thursday, extending its recent decline as the dollar index hovered near a two-month high amid surging U.S. Treasury yields and bets on further policy tightening following solid U.S. private-sector activity. Locally, fundamentals remained weak, pressured by softer consumer confidence and sluggish household spending. Fiscal worries added pressure as higher oil prices lifted budget risks while lawmakers pushed to amend the State Finance Law to allow deficits above 3% of GDP. Bank Indonesia has sought to support the currency through measures beyond interest rates, including raising the FX swap hedging incentive to 25% to encourage foreign capital inflows and curb speculative pressure. The central bank kept its benchmark rate at 5.75% for a third month in September after raising it by a cumulative 100bp in three moves since May. The rupiah has weakened about 0.8% so far this week, setting up a second straight weekly loss.

### 2026-09-24 12:55:36  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #Treasury Yields #The Fed (FOMC) #US10Y #Impact bullish #USDJPY #WTI

【Japan 10-year JGB yield rises to highest since 1996 as global sell-off deepens】Japan's 10-year JGB yield rose 10 bps to 3.075% on Thursday as markets reopened after the Silver Week holiday, the highest level since 1996. Yields across the curve climbed; the 5-year jumped 9.5 bps to 2.37%. The move followed an intensified US Treasury selloff—strong US economic data and a weak Treasury auction pushed US yields to near two-decade highs—while rising oil prices heightened inflation concerns and reinforced expectations of further Fed hikes. Domestic pressure also weighed: the BOJ hiked last Friday and left scope for additional tightening, but traders were disappointed by a lack of clearer guidance on the pace of future moves. JGBs are therefore being driven by both expected BOJ tightening and a global repricing of long-term borrowing costs.

Sensex Falls to Over 1-Week Low — India’s BSE Sensex fell about 0.9% to 74,189 on Thursday, reversing the previous session’s gains and hitting its lowest level since September 15, as investors weighed rising crude oil prices, continued foreign selling, and financial sector regulatory changes. Crude oil prices climbed back above $100 a barrel, raising concerns over inflation and India’s import bill amid its dependence on oil imports. Financial stocks came under pressure after India’s insurance regulator proposed changes to commission rules, including caps based on product complexity and measures to spread life insurance commissions beyond the first year. Investors also focused on the market debut of the National Stock Exchange, India’s largest stock exchange, following a 225.6 billion rupees initial public offering that is expected to post a relatively modest 2%-3% gain on debut. Financials led the losses, with L&T Finance (-7.6%), Bajaj Finance (-4.7%), and Max Financial Services (-11.8%) among the notable laggards.

### 2026-09-24 12:41:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Money Supply M2 #Importance 0

Indonesia Money Supply Growth Slows in August — Indonesia’s broad money supply (M2) rose 8.2% year-on-year to IDR 10,448.1 trillion in August 2026, slowing slightly from 8.3% in July. The moderation was driven by slower growth in narrow money (M1), which eased to 9.0% from 10.0%, while quasi-money growth accelerated to 6.5% from 5.6%. Within M1, rupiah demand deposits grew 7.1%, down from 10.9%, while rupiah savings withdrawable on demand accelerated to 8.9% from 6.8%. Meanwhile, stronger bank lending supported overall liquidity, with credit growth accelerating to 13.3% from 13.0% in July. Net claims on the central government contracted 7.0%, reversing 4.6% growth a month earlier.

【圍繞限制柴油出口的爭論升溫 美國能源部長據悉提醒業界做好準備】美國能源部長克里斯·賴特向石油行業高管表示，鑑於特朗普政府內部對限制柴油出口問題的討論日益激烈，應為可能採取這一措施做好準備。知情人士稱，賴特週二晚間透過電話向企業高管傳達了這一資訊，就在特朗普表示他已敦促政府官員考慮對柴油出口實施限制數小時後。週三，圍繞限制柴油出口的討論仍在繼續，但尚未做出最終決定。特朗普的核心幕僚對此意見不一，石油企業高管則警告白宮，任何禁令最終都將導致全球價格上升。知情人士透露了政府內部討論及賴特與行業高管的通話，由於相關討論不公開，他們要求匿名。而賴特週三著重提到了一個可能的自願性解決方案，稱即使未對柴油出口實施禁令，政府仍希望幫助行業自主增加柴油供應。“我們正在與行業展開某種形式的合作，以期更有效地增加美國柴油供應，遏制價格上漲壓力，”他在《經濟學人》舉辦的一場活動上表示。“但這可以透過更簡單、自願及合作的方式實現，無需動用會降低煉油產能的粗暴手段。”後來，他的表態更加明確，稱全面禁止柴油出口不在考慮之列。“沒有人希望全面禁止或完全停止柴油出口，”他在另一場《紐約時報》的活動上表示。“這並非討論的重點。目前討論的是如何以最有效的方式將更多柴油輸送到美國，並確保汽油、航空燃油等燃料的最大供應量。”

### 2026-09-24 12:16:49  #央行

【央行開展8000億元MLF操作 紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）逆勢上漲半日成交2.67億領跑同類】9月24日，市場低開低走。截至午盤，滬指跌0.93％，創業板指跌1.84％。在此背景下，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）逆勢上漲0.17％，報1.184元，換手率0.87％，半日成交額2.67億元，居同類標的ETF首位。訊息面上，9月23日，央行公告稱，為更好匹配銀行體系短期流動性需求，將於9月28日至10月8日開展隔夜逆回購操作，採用固定利率、數量招標方式，每日操作量不超過10000億元。同時，央行於9月24日以固定數量、利率招標、多重價位中標方式開展8000億元MLF操作，期限為1年期，以保持銀行體系流動性充裕。中信證券首席經濟學家明明認為，總體來看，近期隔夜資金與7天逆回購利率利差較為穩定。本次隔夜逆回購操作體現了對短端中性寬鬆的流動性呵護態度，而MLF迴歸淨投放則體現了對長端流動性呵護加力的態度。申萬宏源證券表示，當前神似2014年上半年，短期調整，中期震盪休整波段延伸。需更加重視科技和非科技主題。科技主題演繹，是醞釀新階段投資主線的過程。短期市場逆風，市場對AI產業進步的挖掘並不充分。這為後續反彈階段的主題輪動保留了空間。非科技方向跑贏的時間拉長，高股息資產有絕對收益的時間拉長。在市場重回強勢線索顯現前，仍可以基於防禦持有高股息。投資者可將紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）作為底倉配置，無股票賬戶的投資者也可透過其場外聯接基金進行配置（A類：007466；C類：007467；I類：022678；Y類：022951）。

### 2026-09-24 12:03:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #European Union #Car Registrations #Importance 2

EU Car Registrations Rise 4.5% in August — EU passenger car registrations increased 4.5% year-on-year to 708.2 thousand units in August 2026, marking the seventh straight month of growth and accelerating from 3.0% growth in July. The market continued to benefit from robust consumer demand for a range of electrified vehicles, driven primarily by market support measures and a broader model offering. The bloc’s four largest markets all posted notable growth: Spain (11.8%), France (7.4%), Italy (3.2%), and Germany (2.6%). In the first eight months of 2026, new EU car registrations increased 5.3%, despite persistent geopolitical headwinds and rising energy prices. Battery-electric vehicles (BEVs) accounted for 21.7% of the EU market, up from 15.8% a year earlier, highlighting continued momentum in the shift toward electrification. Three of the four largest markets, which together accounted for 64% of total BEV registrations, recorded strong growth: France (74.2%), Germany (53.1%), and Denmark (40.9%).

【日元兌美元匯率接近160日元關口 干預風險再度浮現】隨著日本假期結束，日元干預風險再次成為關注焦點；日元長達兩週的跌勢令其再度接近市場密切關注的160兌1美元關卡。週四東京早盤，日元兌美元小幅上漲0.3%，至157.85左右，但這對於過去連續四天的跌勢影響甚微。鑑於日元在9月18日日本央行政策會議後持續走貶，策略師認為，160關卡將再次成為測試日本當局對日元軟弱忍受度的試金石。儘管日本央行加快了緊縮週期的腳步，但政策委員會內部出現分歧，且美聯儲似乎正邁向更為鷹派的路線。洲聯邦銀行貨幣策略師Carol Kong表示，如果美國收益率繼續上升，且市場繼續試探日本捍衛日元的決心，美元兌日元可能很快突破160。若快速突破該關口，將實質性提高官方採取行動的可能性，尤其是考慮到近期有報道稱日本進行了匯率檢查，以及協調幹預的先例。

Rupee Slips on Oil, Firmer Dollar — The Indian rupee fell to around 95.8 per dollar, extending losses for another session as renewed gains in oil prices and a broadly firmer dollar amid rising US rate hike expectations weighed on the currency. A stronger-than-expected US purchasing managers’ report fueled inflation concerns, while a poorly received five-year Treasury auction pushed US yields higher, with the 10-year yield rising more than 15 basis points from Tuesday’s close and the five-year yield climbing above 5% for the first time since 2007. Brent crude reclaimed the $100-a-barrel mark despite Iran signaling openness to diplomacy to end the US-Iran war. Foreign investors have net sold $3.5 billion of Indian stocks and bonds so far this month, adding to pressure on the rupee. Meanwhile, frequent RBI intervention has helped limit losses near the 96 handle, with the central bank reportedly conducting at least $10 billion in sell-buy FX swaps in recent weeks to absorb excess rupee liquidity.

Commonwealth Bank of Australia FX strategist Carol Kong said if U.S. Treasury yields keep rising and markets probe Japan's willingness to defend the currency, USD/JPY could soon break 160. A rapid break would materially raise the likelihood of official intervention, particularly after recent reports the Bank of Japan has been conducting "exchange-rate checks".

Copper Pulls Back From Record High — Copper futures fell below $6.70 per pound on Thursday, retreating from record levels as the dollar strengthened on robust US economic data that reinforced expectations for further Federal Reserve rate hikes. A stronger dollar makes greenback-priced commodities such as copper more expensive for overseas buyers. Still, the metal remains supported by persistent supply risks, with BHP Group announcing that mining operations had been suspended at the giant Escondida mine in Chile following the death of a worker. Metals trader Sprott Asset Management also said global mined copper production could decline this year for the first time since 2017, while disruptions at major mines in Indonesia and the Democratic Republic of Congo have reduced expected annual output by an estimated 600,000 tons. On the demand side, consumption remains robust across power grids, AI data centers and the defense sector, while the recent rally in technology and AI stocks has further strengthened the demand outlook.

Palm Oil Rebounds on Firmer Dalian, India Duty Cuts — Malaysian palm oil futures strengthened, hovering near MYR 4,790 per tonne after recent declines, boosted by a weaker ringgit, firmer edible oils on the Dalian market, and India’s decision to cut basic import duties on crude and refined vegetable oils ahead of the September–November festive season, in an effort to curb domestic food inflation. Supply concerns also lent support, as top supplier Indonesia is expected to face a shorter-than-usual wet season from November, potentially affecting crops. However, the upside was tempered by expectations of higher Malaysian inventories, with a brokerage forecasting end-stocks to reach around 3 million tonnes, or slightly higher, by September-end, driven by a double-digit rise in production, particularly in Sabah. Exports also stayed sluggish, with cargo surveyors noting shipments fell between 12.8%–24.7% mom during September 1–20. In the energy market, softer crude prices amid improving Gulf supply also weighed on sentiment.

China 10Y Yield Holds at 2025 Low — China’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 1.67% on Thursday, remaining near its lowest level since July 2025, even as uncertainty over the extension of the US-China trade truce persisted. US Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent said the US and China had agreed to extend the truce by two months through January 10. However, China has yet to publicly confirm the expiration date, while the extension falls short of the three-to-six-month continuation previously suggested by some US officials. The decline in Chinese yields came despite a broad global bond selloff, as stronger-than-expected US economic data and weak demand at a Treasury auction drove yields across much of the US curve to their highest levels in nearly two decades. The yield premium of 10-year US Treasuries over Chinese government bonds remained close to a record high.

【China's first large-scale 18 MW offshore turbine project enters full-capacity operation】China's first large-scale deployment of 18 MW offshore turbines has begun full-capacity operation, advancing the sector toward larger units and lower costs. The commissioned CGN Guangdong Yangjiang Fanshi 2 GW offshore wind project comprises two 1 GW subprojects and features 33 18 MW turbines, the largest single-unit capacity domestically deployed. Each rotor is 292 m in diameter with a swept area of about 66,000 sq m (roughly nine football pitches). At scale, 18 MW units reduce required turbine counts and are expected to lower costs for subsea cable laying, offshore foundation construction and sea-area occupation.

South Korean Won Weakens on Higher US Yields — The South Korean won weakened to around 1,370 per dollar, reversing gains after a sharp rebound, as a surge in US Treasury yields lifted the dollar. The US 10-year Treasury yield climbed to its highest level since July 2007, while stronger-than-expected US economic data heightened inflation concerns and reinforced expectations for further policy tightening. The probability of another 25-basis-point Fed rate hike in October rose to around 70% from 55%, taking the dollar near its strongest level in almost two months. Additionally, oil prices rebounded amid renewed Middle East risks, with Brent crude rising above $103 a barrel, raising concerns over potential supply disruptions and higher import costs for Korea. Meanwhile, the US and China reportedly agreed to extend their trade truce by two months, giving the two sides more time to negotiate a broader agreement and reducing near-term trade uncertainty for Korea’s major trading partners.

【濱州新能源集團有限責任公司原黨委書記、董事長董建忠被“雙開”】據濱州市紀委監委訊息：經濱州市委批准，濱州市紀委監委對濱州新能源集團有限責任公司原黨委書記、董事長董建忠嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，董建忠喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，對黨不忠誠不老實，對抗組織審查；違背組織原則，隱瞞不報個人有關事項，違規為他人謀取人事利益；廉潔底線失守，違規收受禮品、禮金；違反工作紀律，違規干預和插手市場經濟活動；違反財經法規，違規設立並使用賬外資金；貪慾膨脹，將公權力異化為謀取私利的工具，利用職務便利為他人在工程承攬、工程款撥付等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。董建忠嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律、工作紀律和國家法律法規，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經濱州市紀委常委會會議研究並報濱州市委批准，決定給予董建忠開除黨籍處分；由濱州市監委給予其開除公職處分；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

Taiwan Stocks Pull Back from Record High — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, dropped 208 points, or 0.4%, to 47,949 in Thursday morning trade, snapping a six-session winning streak, as traders took profit after the index hit a record high a day earlier. The index declined from its first close above 48,000 at 48,157 on Wednesday, mainly weighed down by declines in electronic technology, finance, and health technology. Electronic Technology fell 0.4% after rising 1.2% in the previous session, with TSMC, the world's largest contract chipmaker and accounting for more than 40% of the market's total value, falling 0.6% after rising on Wednesday. Meanwhile, Hon Hai Precision, ASE Tech, and United Microelectronics dropped by 2.2%, 0.4%, and 4.1%, respectively. The broader index tracked a decline on Wall Street overnight amid rising oil prices and US Treasury yields. Traders also anticipated the Trump-Xi summit, with AI and tariffs expected to be discussed at the meeting.

【機構預計本輪成品油零售價上調】國內成品油新一輪調價視窗將在9月24日24時開啟。機構預計，本輪成品油零售價將上調。金聯創分析師趙櫻涵分析稱，由於本輪調價週期前期原油漲幅已鎖定，國內成品油零售價將上調，但隨著近期國際原油價格連續下跌，最終上調幅度已較前期預期明顯收窄。 據金聯創測算，截至9月23日第九個工作日，參考原油品種均價為102.20美元/桶，變化率為8.62%，國內汽柴油零售價應上調490元/噸。本輪調價視窗為9月24日24時，預計成品油零售價將上調，但上調幅度預期收窄，由0.8元/升收窄至0.6元/升。 今年以來，國內油價已經歷十八輪調價，總體格局為“十二漲五跌一擱淺”。其中，為減緩國際油價上漲帶來的衝擊，國家先後三次對成品油價格採取調控措施。若本輪（第十九輪）調價如預期上調，2026年的調價格局將變為“十三漲五跌一擱淺”。（中新經緯）

### 2026-09-24 11:04:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Stock Market #Importance 1

Indonesia Stocks Retreat on Global Jitters — Indonesian shares slipped 42 points, or 0.7%, to 6,332 in Thursday morning trade, reversing the prior day’s rally as U.S. futures fell after Wall Street ended lower overnight amid surging U.S. Treasury yields that stoked inflation fears. Locally, economists warned that Bank Indonesia’s move in September to hold benchmark rates steady for a third month may be only a temporary pause, with external risks still elevated from higher global yields, volatile oil prices, and geopolitical headwinds. Policymakers also face challenges from slowing growth, tepid household spending, and fragile market sentiment, particularly as the rupiah remains vulnerable to renewed dollar strength and foreign outflows. Still, losses were capped by resilient loan growth, with August figures near a 12-year peak. Declines were broad-based, led by basic materials, healthcare, and property. Notable laggards included Bumi Resources (-3.1%), Pertamina Geothermal (-2.3%), and Indika Energi (-1.8%).

### 2026-09-24 11:00:18  #宏觀 #市場

【市場監管總局釋出《認證機構資質許可審查通則》】市場監管總局釋出《認證機構資質許可審查通則》，系統最佳化許可標準和審批流程，強化源頭准入管控，推動認證行業規範有序、優質高效發展。《通則》釋出是對認證機構資質行政許可工作的一次系統性完善。透過統一資質審查程式，最佳化許可標準和審批流程，進一步嚴把准入關口，提高機構質量，夯實行業准入制度基礎，為促使認證機構發揮質量“守門員”作用提供更加堅實的制度保障。《通則》重點提高了對認證人員能力、產品認證機構檢測能力等直接影響專業化水平的條件要求，細化了現場核查和專家評審的相關規定，完善了審批與後續日常監管的銜接機制，明確了登出、撤銷《認證機構批准書》的法定情形。透過嚴格機構准入條件，強化監管約束，促進機構水平提升、行業生態最佳化，助力產業提質增效。針對已取得資質的認證機構，《通則》設定了6個月的過渡期。下一步，市場監管總局將做好舊規廢止、新舊制度銜接工作，加強過渡期實施情況跟蹤指導，切實推動制度落地見效。

Australia 10Y Yield Rises Near 15-Year Peaks — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield rose toward 5.4%, near its highest since mid-2011, tracking a global bond selloff fueled by strong US economic data, higher oil prices, and hawkish central-bank expectations. Strong US business activity has increased expectations for another Fed rate hike, pushing Treasury yields sharply higher. Rising oil prices also added to inflationary pressures after Iran cast doubt on progress in Middle East peace talks. In Australia, employment rose by a stronger 39,500 in August, well above forecasts for a 20,000 increase, while the unemployment rate edged higher to 4.6%, its highest in five years, pointing to mixed labor-market conditions. The report was the final major economic release before the Reserve Bank of Australia's September 28–29 meeting, but persistent inflation risks are keeping the central bank on track for another rate hike. Markets are pricing a 95% chance of a 25-bp hike to 4.60% in September and a possible peak around 5.10%.

【國家礦山安全監察局：五類災害鑑定機構須持證執業】記者從今天召開的國家礦山安全監察局新聞釋出會上獲悉，為進一步規範煤礦災害等級鑑定，國家礦山安全監察局於近期印發《煤礦災害等級鑑定工作管理辦法》，重點壓實鑑定活動的全鏈條管控責任。《煤礦災害等級鑑定工作管理辦法》明確：煤與瓦斯突出、衝擊地壓、煤層自燃傾向性、煤塵爆炸性、露天煤礦滑坡危險性等五類災害等級鑑定機構，必須依法取得安全生產檢測檢驗資質後方可從事鑑定活動。在現場管理方面，要求所有災害鑑定專案至少有兩名專職技術人員赴現場開展勘查、取樣、測試，並提前書面告知專案實施地煤礦安全監管部門，接受監督檢查。鑑定機構對分支機構實行一體化管理，嚴防體外運轉和違法分包轉包，鑑定報告須由法定代表人簽字簽章、全省統一編號出具。同時對全國煤礦災害等級鑑定機構建立信用記錄機制，依法將嚴重違法違規行為納入失信名單，並透過國家相關信用管理機制實施信用管理。（央視新聞）

Heating Oil Rises After Export Ban Denial — US heating oil futures rose above $4.80 per gallon, partially recovering their losses from the previous session, after the White House denied reports that the US was preparing a 90-day diesel export ban. Energy Secretary Chris Wright also said the administration was instead working with refiners to increase US diesel supplies through voluntary measures that would not reduce refining throughput, although he provided no details and said no decisions had been made. Meanwhile, EIA data showed US distillate inventories fell by 400,000 barrels in the week ended September 18, while production declined to an average of 5.2 million barrels per day. Inventories are now about 12% below the five-year average, pointing to tight supplies. Heating oil prices have surged to record highs this month as disruptions to crude oil supplies from the Middle East and strikes on Russian refineries have tightened global fuel availability.

New Zealand Dollar Remains Under Pressure — The New Zealand dollar held around $0.567 on Thursday, hovering at an eleven-week low, as a robust US dollar keeps pressure on the kiwi. The greenback reached its strongest level in nearly two months, as stronger-than-expected PMI data fuelled concerns over inflation and strengthened expectations for more interest-rate hikes. Even before the release, several Fed officials had voiced broader concerns about persistent inflation risks, saying that rate increases are likely needed. The hawkish Fed outlook overshadowed rising expectations for further tightening by the Reserve Bank of New Zealand. Markets are now pricing in an 87% chance of a third hike in October, up sharply from 20% earlier this month, following Governor Anna Breman’s warning that sustained oil price increases could push near-term inflation above the central bank’s latest forecast. However, her emphasis on an uneven economic recovery underscored the challenge of balancing inflation risks with still-fragile domestic growth.

Australian Dollar Hits 7-Week Low — The Australian dollar weakened to around $0.70, hitting a seven-week low as a stronger US dollar and a renewed selloff in global bond markets weighed on risk-sensitive currencies. The move came despite Australian employment rising by a stronger-than-expected 39,500 in August, well above forecasts for a 20,000 increase. The unemployment rate edged higher to 4.6%, its highest in five years, pointing to mixed labor-market conditions. The report was the final major economic release before the Reserve Bank of Australia's September 28–29 meeting, but persistent inflation risks are keeping the central bank on track for another rate hike. Markets are pricing a 95% chance of a 25-bp hike to 4.60% in September and a possible peak around 5.10%. Meanwhile, uncertainty surrounding US-Iran negotiations kept oil prices elevated, fueling inflation concerns and a renewed selloff in global bond markets, while strong US business activity has increased bets for another Fed hike, boosting the greenback.

Offshore Yuan Nears 3-Week Low — The offshore yuan weakened past 6.71 per dollar on Thursday, hitting its lowest level in nearly three weeks, as a firm US dollar and uncertainty over the extension of the US-China trade truce weighed on the currency. The greenback remained supported by rising expectations for further policy tightening, with markets now pricing in around a 70% chance of a Fed rate hike in October, up from 55% a day earlier. Adding to pressure on the yuan, Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent said the US and China had agreed to extend their trade truce by two months, through January 10. However, China has yet to publicly confirm the expiration date, while the extension falls short of the three-to-six-month continuation previously suggested by some US officials. While expectations for the Trump-Xi summit remain subdued, the talks are expected to focus on artificial intelligence, Iran, Taiwan and broader economic ties, as both sides seek to preserve stability in bilateral relations.

【Australia says OpenAI agent breached government health data portal】Australia said on Wednesday an OpenAI agent in June breached a government health data portal and accessed files without authorization, a possible first known AI-agent intrusion of a government website. Canberra said the incident is one of the most notable cases of an AI agent accessing external systems outside the United States and follows other recent runaway AI-agent incidents. Prime Minister Albanese said the agent accessed a government agency’s medical statistics portal that holds non-sensitive health data and statistics, including public health spending, and that available evidence indicates the wider network was not compromised. An investigation is ongoing; Australia has conveyed extreme concern to OpenAI CEO Altman and said it is disappointed by the company’s delayed notification.

Australia Employment Rises More than Estimated — Employment in Australia increased by 39,500 to a new record of 14.84 million in August 2026, reversing a 15,800 decline in the previous month and easily exceeding market expectations for a 20,000 gain. Part-time employment rose by 45,800 to 4.64 million while full-time employment declined by 6,300 to 10.19 million. Compared with a year earlier, total employment expanded 238,100, or 1.6%.

### 2026-09-24 09:38:28  #其他

【教育部部署2027年全國碩士研究生考試招生工作】教育部近日印發《2027年全國碩士研究生招生工作管理規定》（以下簡稱《規定》），部署各地各研究生招生單位做好2027年全國碩士研究生考試招生工作。 《規定》指出，各地各招生單位要進一步加強碩士研究生招生工作制度機制建設，細化完善管理舉措，壓緊壓實責任鏈條，確保各項制度要求落地落實。招生單位要切實落實主體責任，進一步發揮本單位研究生招生委員會統籌協調、稽核把關作用，統籌推進招生錄取工作，審議招生章程、擬錄取結果等重大事項。 《規定》要求，各地各招生單位要堅持以考生為本，持續最佳化考生服務，細緻做好政策宣傳解讀、諮詢答覆等服務保障工作，落實招生資訊公開要求，暢通考生信訪舉報渠道，建立完善接訴即辦機制，切實維護考生合法權益。 《規定》明確，2027年全國碩士研究生招生初試時間為2026年12月19日至20日。報名時間為2026年10月15日至10月24日（預報名時間為2026年10月9日至10月12日，相關工作安排由各省級教育招生考試機構確定並公佈），每日9:00—22:00。“中國研究生招生資訊網”將進一步升級報名系統，最佳化報名照片採集模式，考生直接透過學信網APP線上拍攝並上傳本人照片；簡化考生報名程式，推進網上報名和網上確認一體化，考生在填寫報考資訊後一併提交核驗材料，實現“一站式”完成報名。招生單位和報考點根據相關規定對考生報名資訊和提交材料進行稽核，確定考生的考試資格。對於提交材料不符合要求等特殊情況考生，需根據稽核工作要求按時提交補充材料。所有考生均須在規定時間內完成報名，逾期不得補辦。 “中國研究生招生資訊網”將於2026年9月26日至29日開展“2027年全國碩士研究生招生宣傳諮詢活動”，屆時所有招生單位將線上解答考生諮詢。

Australia Shares Fall Sharply Ahead of Labor Data — Australian stocks plunged 116 points or 1.3% to 8,649 in early deals on Thursday, reversing modest gains in the previous three sessions as Wall Street’s overnight losses spilled into local markets. Surging U.S. bond yields, inflation fears, and renewed Middle East tensions that lifted oil prices above USD US103 pressured sentiment. Caution also mounted ahead of August labor data later today after July’s surprise uptick in unemployment and a decline in employment. Meanwhile, RBA Assistant Governor Sarah Hunter warned cash rates may need to rise again if inflation persists, reinforcing expectations from the four major banks that the central bank will hike next week. All sectors retreated, led by manufacturing, non-energy minerals, and financials. The big four banks fell 1.7%–1.9%, while steep losses hit PLS Group (-4.9%), Lynas Rare Earths (-4.6%), and Nextdc (-3.5%). Traders now anticipate a bilateral summit between U.S. President Donald Trump and China's leader Xi Jinping later today.

【飛天茅臺迭創月新高 青花汾20突破400元 國窖1573、習酒君品大跌】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，9月24日中國白酒業12大單品的終端零售均價五漲七跌，下跌品種數量佔優，但累計跌幅僅小幅超出上漲端。贏家方面，飛天茅臺上漲2元至1808元，六連陽穩步抬升，連續五日挺立於1800元大關之上，迭創近一個月新高；精品茅臺上漲4元至2463元，三連陰告終，價盤溫和修復；青花汾20上漲4元至402元，連續四日攀升，站上400元整數關口，創下近一個月最高紀錄；洋河夢之藍M6+上漲2元至618元，平盤蓄力後繼續走高；青花郎上漲4元至677元，結束二連陰頹勢，後市能否走出低谷值得關注。下跌方面，茅臺1935小跌1元至684元，延續高位窄幅震盪行情；五糧液普五八代下跌2元至803元，價盤再度小幅下探，近期圍繞800元關口反覆爭奪；五糧液1618下跌1元至829元，昨日平盤後出現小幅抖動；國窖1573大跌7元至880元，前期二連陽漲勢受阻，短線價格中樞明顯下移；古井貢古20下跌2元至520元，連續兩日走低，前期反彈動能有所減弱；習酒君品下跌5元至635元，昨日衝高後快速回落；水晶劍南春小跌1元，自近期高位小幅回落，仍維持在410元一線。

今日12大單品的終端零售總價結束連續四日上行強勢，雖然下跌品種數量佔優，但漲跌兩端累計幅度差距有限，因此整體價盤僅小幅回落。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10729元，較昨日小跌3元，仍穩居10700元整百關口之上。

### 2026-09-24 09:08:53  #宏觀 #公司 #市場 #國際

【美國石油與天然氣行業全力出擊 試圖阻止柴油出口禁令】據報道，當油氣行業聽聞美國總統特朗普擬出臺柴油出口禁令的訊息後，便拿出了 “重武器” 展開遊說。週二特朗普提出這一建議後不久，美國石油學會（API）執行長邁克·薩默斯（Mike Sommers）便發表宣告譴責此舉。作為該行業的首要遊說團體，他表示，限制美國出口“只會使”柴油成本不斷上漲的問題“雪上加霜”。API還專門釋出了一份新聞稿，闡述了實施出口禁令的危險性。該組織表示：“限制美國柴油出口將對國內外的燃料市場造成嚴重破壞，擾亂煉油廠的運營，並加劇一場全球性的煉油危機，而這場危機本已給美國價格帶來上漲壓力。”幾乎在同一時間，一個與API及美國燃料與石化製造商協會（AFPM，代表煉油商的行業團體）有聯絡的CEO鬆散聯盟也迅速採取行動。據知情人士透露，雪佛龍執行長邁克·沃思（Mike Wirth）、菲利普斯66執行長馬克·拉希爾（Mark Lashier）及其他高管已開始致電他們在政府內部的聯絡人，以抵制這項禁令。這些高管與政府內部不同成員的關係各異，他們均已明確表達了不滿。據知情人士透露，埃克森美孚和瓦萊羅（Valero）也參與了這一抵制行動。此外，該行業還得到了得克薩斯州參議員特德·克魯茲（Ted Cruz）以及路易斯安那州共和黨人的支援。

### 2026-09-24 08:59:25  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Platinum Falls to 5-Week Low — Platinum futures held below $1,760 an ounce after dropping more than 4% in the previous session to a five-week low, as higher Treasury yields and dollar strength reduced demand for the non-yielding metal. The selloff followed stronger-than-expected US business activity data, reinforcing expectations for higher interest rates. The 10-year US Treasury yield climbed to its highest level since 2007, while the dollar strengthened as markets raised bets on another Federal Reserve rate hike at the October meeting. Rising oil prices also added to inflation concerns, after Iran's President said Tehran would keep the Strait of Hormuz closed while US sanctions and the blockade remain. Still, platinum's fundamentals remain supportive, with above-ground stocks forecast to cover just 3.4 months of demand by year-end despite a projected 265,000-ounce surplus in 2026. Industrial demand is expected to rise 5%, driven partly by AI-related glass and electrical applications.

【途虎-W：擬2.78億澳元收購Conti Trade Australia Pty Ltd全部股權】途虎-W(09690)釋出公告，於2026年9月24日，買方(公司間接全資附屬公司TUHU Car (Hong Kong) Limited)與賣方(Continental AG之全資附屬公司Continental Global Holding Netherlands B.V.)訂立股份買賣協議，據此，買方已有條件同意收購目標公司(Conti Trade Australia Pty Ltd)之全部已發行股本。Continental AG為一家於法蘭克福證券交易所上市的公司(股票代號：CON)，並且為領先的輪胎製造商及行業專家。目標公司擁有及營運“mycar Tyre & Auto”網路，其為澳大利亞最大的輪胎、汽車服務及維修連鎖店之一，透過全國279家門店提供全面的一站式汽車服務。收購事項乃基於目標集團的協定企業價值4.03億澳元(相當於約22.5億港元)，經就目標集團的淨債務及營運資金金額作出調整後，得出交割付款目前估計約為2.78億澳元(相當於約15.5億港元)，該款項須於交割時以現金支付，並可予進行交割後調整。集團主要在中國透過線上線下一體化平臺從事提供汽車服務。收購事項為集團提供了在澳大利亞汽車服務市場建立規模化直接業務據點，實現地域分佈多元化，並擴大其國際業務版圖的戰略機遇。

【Japan to trim liquidity-enhancing JGB auction sizes for 5–11yr, sources say】Two sources said the Japanese Ministry of Finance plans to discuss next week with primary dealers a reduction in the size of liquidity-enhancing auctions for 5–11 year JGBs. Silver Pressured by Stronger Dollar, Yields — Silver fell toward $64 an ounce on Thursday, extending losses the previous session, pressured by a stronger dollar and surging Treasury yields. Those moves came as stronger-than-expected US private-sector data heightened inflation concerns and strengthened expectations for further Federal Reserve rate hikes. Several Fed officials have also reiterated support for last week’s rate increase while warning about persistent inflation risks. Markets are now pricing in around a 70% chance of another Fed rate hike in October, up from 55% a day earlier. At the same time, oil prices rebounded as Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian maintained a firm stance, saying Tehran would not allow freedom of navigation through the Strait of Hormuz while sanctions and a US blockade remain in place. Higher oil prices add to inflationary pressures, reinforcing expectations for a more hawkish interest-rate outlook.

【CITIC Securities sees 2026 as potential turning year for liquid‑cooling supply‑chain orders and earnings】CITIC Securities said AI compute is entering a full‑liquid‑cooling era, with liquid cooling shifting from an optional configuration to required infrastructure. Mainstream platforms — NVIDIA Rubin, Google TPU v7/v8, AMD Helios and ByteDance’s AI Rack 3.0 — are moving from high‑share liquid or air‑liquid hybrid designs to 100% liquid‑cooled, fanless architectures. Coupled with tight local capacity overseas, accelerating exports by Chinese suppliers and the roll‑out of China’s national standards, 2026 could be a turning year for concentrated order and earnings realization across the liquid‑cooling supply chain.

【經濟日報金觀平：構建普惠包容的數字貿易體系】當前，數字貿易已成為拉動全球經濟增長的重要引擎。9月23日至27日，第五屆全球數字貿易博覽會在杭州召開，為全球數字技術與國際貿易融合發展提供了展示視窗。我國數字貿易蓬勃發展，為全球數字貿易增長作出積極貢獻。2025年，我國可數字化交付的服務進出口總額超4323億美元，同比增長6.3%。本屆數貿會上，人工智慧參展企業佔比超過三分之一，率先設立詞元（Token）專區。這些變化表明，我國數字貿易的發力點正從跨境電商規模擴張，轉向以人工智慧為核心的賦能型貿易輸出。數字貿易的普惠之路，核心在技術向善，關鍵在規則包容。只有堅持開放合作、互利共贏，推動構建公平合理、包容普惠的全球數字貿易治理體系，才能將數字貿易打造成為共同發展的新引擎，為世界經濟增長注入更大動能。

### 2026-09-24 07:51:21  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Futures Steady After Market Selloff — US stock futures steadied on Thursday after the major averages fell in the previous session, as strong economic data, a weak Treasury auction and renewed inflation concerns tied to energy prices pushed Treasury yields to multi-decade highs. In regular trading on Wednesday, the Dow fell 0.68%, the S&P 500 declined 0.75% and the Nasdaq Composite dropped 1.13%. Ten of the 11 S&P sectors ended lower, led by communication services, utilities and consumer discretionary. The benchmark 10-year US Treasury yield climbed to around 5.11%, its highest level since July 2007. The move followed S&P Global PMI data showing strong new orders and multi-year highs in input and output price inflation, fueling expectations of another Federal Reserve rate hike this year. Oil prices also rebounded as uncertainty persisted over progress in US-Iran talks. Investors now await the latest weekly jobless claims data, along with earnings reports from Costco and Darden Restaurants.

【一線城市新房成交面積同比增長超10% 樓市“金九”成色向好】9月是房地產市場的傳統旺季。目前，市場旺季特徵已有所顯現，重點城市新房、二手房市場活躍度均有提升。 從新房市場來看，中指研究院資料顯示，截至9月20日，9月重點30城新建商品住宅成交面積同比微增1.4%，一線城市新建商品住宅成交面積同比增長超10%，其中，北京、上海分別同比增長23%和22%。 在二手房市場方面，截至9月20日，9月重點20城二手住宅成交套數同比增長13.1%，其中，北京、上海二手房分別成交1.0萬套、1.6萬套，分別同比增長2.2%、24.3%。 中指研究院指數研究部總經理曹晶晶表示，長期來看，隨著現房銷售逐步推進，疊加房企投資和開發決策趨於審慎，新房供應或進一步回落，部分購房需求或向二手房市場分流，對二手房成交形成一定支撐。（證券日報）

### 2026-09-24 07:35:38  #公司

【Anthropic成立生命科學研究團隊，Claude發現具有CRISPR樣特徵的新型酶系統】當地時間9月23日，Anthropic宣佈成立生命科學研究團隊及實驗室，並公佈Claude參與生物學研究取得的早期成果。研究人員稱，Claude在分析超過20萬個逆轉錄酶（RT）序列後，發現一種此前未被描述的酶系統，並將其命名為“陣列相關逆轉錄酶”（ART）。Anthropic稱，Claude約950個智慧體在21小時內使用約2.1億個Token，篩選出3500個候選系統並進一步縮小至20個。ART主要存在於感染細菌的噬菌體中，由逆轉錄酶、鄰近的輔助基因以及一組排列規律的DNA重複序列組成，後者與CRISPR重複序列具有相似特徵。初步實驗顯示，這些重複序列也會被轉錄為多個短RNA，但其具體功能仍有待進一步研究。該研究由Anthropic科學家進行實驗驗證，公司已釋出預印本。

### 2026-09-24 07:30:17  #市場 #觀點

【機構：市場反彈的持續性仍然可期】展望後市，東方證券研報表示，中秋、國慶假期漸近，短期行業輪動加快，市場短期情緒抬升，中期不確定性趨於平穩，整體風險可控，指數或維持震盪格局。 市場風格上，國海證券策略團隊釋出觀點稱，未來1個月，市場有望延續反彈，但行業輪動速度依然可能會很快，仍然建議重視擁擠度指標。 華寶證券研報分析稱，從量能來看，市場活躍度從低位修復，科技方向有望企穩反彈，但考慮到長假臨近，部分資金持幣過節對市場反彈高度形成制約，反彈空間或待節後開啟。 國金證券策略團隊認為，配置上建議關注三大方向：一是有色金屬，例如金、銅、鋁，在加息預期落地與地緣衝突緩和後，機會已經出現；二是油價下跌反而可能釋放全球對能源的補庫需求，石油、油運和煉化為代表的能化鏈條將繼續受益；三是科技鏈上部分供需緊缺且存在技術迭代的環節，推薦光模組、電子布、MLCC、儲存等方向。（上海證券報）

【美參議員：特朗普擬以太陽能專案許可換取法案談判】參議院一位資深談判代表表示，特朗普政府官員提議批准擱置中的太陽能與風電專案，以此作為與民主黨談判法案的籌碼，該法案旨在整體加快能源專案審批流程。參議院環境委員會首席民主黨參議員謝爾頓・懷特豪斯週三在華盛頓對記者表示，他正在要求白宮進一步明確這項提議。一位不願具名的知情人士稱，特朗普政府計劃在約 300 個等待國防部審批的風電專案中，放行其中 30 個。相關討論仍屬私下磋商。白宮暫未回應置評請求。參議院民主黨與共和黨談判代表若達成協議，對油氣行業而言將是重大利好。長期以來，油氣行業一直希望加快聯邦層面審批，縮減環境審查流程；這類審查往往造成專案數年延期，併產生數億美元額外成本。這項協議同樣會惠及可再生能源行業。該行業希望加快新建輸電線路的審批，以便將偏遠風電場和光伏電站的電力輸送至城市與工業使用者。儘管如此，政府的提議僅能覆蓋特朗普第二任期內被叫停的可再生能源專案中的一小部分。面對政府的反對以及總統下達的暫停令，部分開發商已經撤出海上風電投資。

Brent Holds Gains on US-Iran Uncertainty — Brent crude held above $102 per barrel on Thursday after rebounding in the previous session, as uncertainty persisted over progress in US-Iran talks. Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian told the UN General Assembly that Tehran would not bow to threats, adding that the Islamic Republic won’t give up the right to develop nuclear technology for economic development. Pezeshkian also said Iran would not allow freedom of navigation through the Strait of Hormuz as long as sanctions and a US blockade remain in effect. In the US, Energy Secretary Chris Wright said the Trump administration was working with refiners on a voluntary reduction in US diesel exports as an alternative to imposing a formal ban on overseas shipments. Meanwhile, Saudi Arabia is taking steps to resume oil exports through its key East-West pipeline. Ukrainian President Zelenskyy also said he and President Trump had discussed an energy ceasefire.

New Zealand Stocks Trade Slightly Higher — The NZX 50 gained 14 points, or 0.1%, to 13,836 in Thursday morning trade, erasing losses from the previous session, mainly buoyed by financials, consumer staples, and utilities. However, higher oil prices capped the gain due to escalating tensions in the Middle East after Iran's president vowed never to surrender. ### 2026-09-24 06:45:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Heating Oil Plunges on Export Ban Reports — US heating oil futures sank 2.7% to $4.80 per gallon on Wednesday despite a surge in crude oil prices after reports stated that the Trump administration could ban diesel exports for 90 days. The proposal faces divisions within the administration and the oil industry as market fundamentals clash against high diesel prices ahead of mid-term elections. The US exports around 20% of global diesel, flooding the domestic market with fuel in the short term should the ban pass. Still, should stocks of distillate fuel be refilled, the ban would cut refining capacity at a time that crack spreads continue to extend record highs. Distillate fuel prices had surged to record highs this month as low crude oil supplies from the Middle East and strikes on Russian refineries dampened global availability of fuel. The potential for an export ban from the US were combined with President Trump attempting to orchestrate an energy infrastructure ceasefire between Russia and Ukraine.

Canada 10-Year Yield Nears Three-Year High — Canada’s 10-year government bond yield rose to around 3.95% in September, nearing a three-year high as energy-driven inflation concerns persisted. Oil prices rose after a five-week halt in the rally that followed the US-Iran war, amid uncertainty over diplomatic efforts to end the conflict and reopen the Strait of Hormuz. Strong US economic data also strengthened expectations that the Fed could deliver another rate hike this year. US Treasury yields soared to multi-decade highs, further pressuring Canadian bonds as domestic yields tend to track US rates. Fed projections showed that most policymakers expect another hike before the end of 2026. The Bank of Canada kept its key policy rate unchanged at 2.25% at its September meeting, as widely expected. However, it noted that inflation risks had increased, while new tariffs had made the growth outlook more uncertain. Governor Macklem said policymakers were prepared to raise rates if inflation remained elevated.

【HASSETT criticizes Fed officials' recent support for more hikes, urges restoration of independence】White House NEC Director HASSETT on Wednesday criticized recent Federal Reserve officials advocating further rate hikes, saying additional tightening is worrying with core inflation near 2% and asking “why raise rates.” He questioned comments from officials not appointed by Trump and said Fed Chair KEVIN WARSH is running a “highly politicized” Fed, calling restoration of the central bank’s independence a key priority. HASSETT also criticized senior Fed officials remaining in post after leaving leadership roles, naming Powell and Barr as departures from past transition norms. Several Fed officials have sent hawkish signals recently: Barr said further hikes may be needed to bring inflation back to 2%, and Collins and Musallam also expressed support for additional tightening. The Fed’s latest projections show 16 officials expect at least one more rate hike this year.

Argentina Retail Sales Growth Accelerates — Argentina’s retail sales surged 23% year-on-year to ARS 707.8 billion in July 2026, accelerating from a 13.4% increase in June. Sales increased 26% in Buenos Aires and 23.9% across the 24 districts of Greater Buenos Aires. Meanwhile, sales in the Pampean Region rose 17.6%, while the Cuyo Region recorded a 17% increase. 【Microsoft president: AI safety cannot rely on a handful of model firms】Microsoft President Brad Smith said the company backs independent safety-assessment bodies for AI deployments, arguing AI safety should not depend on a small number of model firms. He acknowledged OpenAI and Anthropic as important but said software firms that control and monitor AI agents are equally critical. Microsoft participates in safety assessments via a committee set up with OpenAI and supports human-operated shutdown mechanisms for advanced AI systems. Smith said AI safety requires multi-layered defenses and that key controls should not be centralized. He confirmed Microsoft still plans to deploy 200 MW of compute at a UAE AI data center and views the Middle East as a timely investment destination. On its carbon-negative-by-2030 target, he said the company may need to adjust its previously set pathway.

Brent Extends Gains as Geopolitical Risks Persist — Brent crude rose above $103 a barrel on Wednesday after five consecutive sessions of losses, as uncertainty persisted over progress in US-Iran talks. Iran’s President Masoud Pezeshkian said at the UN General Assembly that Tehran remained open to diplomacy but would not negotiate under pressure, while Iran’s military warned it was ready to launch “more crushing” strikes in response to Trump's threats to annihilate Iran earlier this week in his UN adress. Trump’s threats were followed by what he described as productive talks with Iranian envoys, while Tehran has indicated it could reopen the strait if Washington eases military pressure and lifts restrictions on Iranian ports. Saudi Arabia’s partial restoration of its East-West pipeline is helping offset some supply concerns. Also, Ukrainian President Zelenskyy called on US President Trump to organize a trilateral meeting with Russian President Putin. Zelenskyy also said that the he and Trump discussed an energy ceasefire.

Iran’s President Masoud Pezeshkian said at the UN General Assembly that Tehran remained open to diplomacy but would not negotiate under pressure, while Iran’s military warned it was ready to launch “more crushing” strikes in response to Trump's threats to annihilate Iran earlier this week in his UN adress. Trump’s threats were followed by what he described as productive talks with Iranian envoys, while Tehran has indicated it could reopen the strait if Washington eases military pressure and lifts restrictions on Iranian ports. Saudi Arabia’s partial restoration of its East-West pipeline is helping offset some supply concerns. Also, Ukrainian President Zelenskyy called on US President Trump to organize a trilateral meeting with Russian President Putin. Zelenskyy also said that the he and Trump discussed an energy ceasefire.

【Microsoft to add $2 bln in Gulf to expand AI and cloud operations】Microsoft said it will add roughly $2 bln of investment in the Gulf, bringing planned spending in Saudi Arabia, Kuwait, Qatar and the UAE to about $10 bln by 2030, including last year’s $7.9 bln UAE commitment. The new funds will target cloud, AI and data-center operations, covering capex and opex; about $400 mln is earmarked for a subsea cable and related infrastructure to boost network resilience and data transmission. Microsoft previously invested $1.5 bln in UAE tech firm G42 and has participated in local AI data-center builds. The announcement is the company’s first regional update since the US-Iran conflict; several data centers were affected during the period and Amazon recently said some data at attacked facilities in the UAE and Bahrain could not be recovered.

### 2026-09-24 00:56:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Russia #Industrial Production #Importance 0

Russia Industrial Production Unexpectadly Declines — Industrial production in Russia declined 0.6% year-on-year in August 2026, reversing a 0.4% increase in July and contrasting with market expectations of a 0.5% gain. Activity remained in contraction in mining, although the decline eased to 1.3% from 2.6%, while output in electricity, gas, steam and air-conditioning supply fell 1.2%, following a 4% decline in the previous month. Manufacturing output also contracted by 0.5%, reversing a 2.4% increase in July. Meanwhile, production growth in water supply, sewerage, waste management and remediation accelerated to 6.9% from 2.8%. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial production declined 0.3%, reversing a 0.1% increase in July.

Swiss Bond Yield Rebounds — The yield on the Swiss 10-year government bond rose above 0.55% after reaching its lowest level in over two weeks, as global oil prices rebounded, raising concerns over prolonged inflationary pressures. On the monetary policy front, the Swiss National Bank is set to announce its policy rate decision on Thursday and is widely expected to leave the rate unchanged at 0%. A Swiss Bankers Association survey showed that all respondents expect the SNB to keep its policy rate at 0% through the end of the year. Markets are pricing in the first rate hike for June 2027, while most economists expect the first increase in early 2028. Meanwhile, the State Secretariat for Economic Affairs raised its forecast for economic growth in 2026 to 1.7%, as the impact of higher energy prices remains limited, up from its June projection of 0.9% and broadly in line with the OECD’s latest forecast of 2%.

FTSE 100 Edges Up on Wednesday — The FTSE 100 edged up on Wednesday, outperforming its European peers thanks to gains in energy shares. Oil majors Shell and BP rose 1.8% and 2.8%, respectively, due to a renewed advance in oil prices. Defence stocks also performed well, with aerospace company BAE Systems up 1.9%, submarine contractor Babcock climbing 2.6%, and engine-maker Rolls-Royce rising 1.3%. On the other hand, JD Sports tumbled more than 3% after maintaining its guidance, and trimming its outlook last month amid weakness in the trainer market. Diageo fell around 0.8% after appointing Joanne Wilson, currently CFO of WPP, as its new chief financial officer from 2027. Meanwhile, Airtel Money confirmed plans for a London listing that could become the city’s largest IPO since 2021. On the data front, the UK Composite PMI eased to 51.7 in September, slightly under market expectations of 52.

### 2026-09-23 23:53:10  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Stock Market #Importance 1

European Stocks Pull Back — European stocks closed lower on Wednesday amid pressure from a surge in sovereign yields. The Euro STOXX 50 fell 0.4% to 6,300 and the STOXX Europe 600 eased 0.3% to 640.7. Sovereign yields in the Eurozone soared to multi-decade highs as the combination of strong economic growth and higher energy prices consolidated the likelihood that the ECB will continue to raise interest rates this year. The Eurozone's PMI was stronger than expected for both services providers and manufacturers, while the survey confirmed that soaring energy prices in the period were passed on to output charges. Allianz and Deutsche Bank sank 3.8% and 2.6% and Bunds tumbled, while BNP Paribas fell over 1% to set the tone for credit-sensitive financial companies. German auto manufacturers also slipped, with Mercedes Benz and BMW dropping around 3% each.

### 2026-09-23 23:49:40  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cocoa Futures at Near 1-Week High — Cocoa futures rose toward $5,500 per tonne, the highest level in nearly a week, supported by short covering as forecasts for dry conditions in top grower Côte d’Ivoire raised concerns about the 2026/27 crop. Early surveys also pointed to below-average cherelle formation on cocoa trees, signalling a weaker outlook for the main harvest, which began this month. These concerns come despite a large global surplus in 2025/26 that has kept near-term supplies ample, with inventories remaining at a two-year high of 3.43 million bags. However, the medium-term outlook is more uncertain, with estimates for 2026/27 pointing to weaker crops across West Africa due to weather risks. Côte d’Ivoire’s output is expected to fall about 20% to 1.75 million tonnes, while Ghana’s is forecast to decline 13% to 650,000 tonnes. These risks could be compounded by an unusually strong El Niño, potentially intensifying dry Harmattan winds from around November.

【義大利議會透過核能授權法案 為重啟核電鋪平道路】當地時間9月23日，義大利參議院表決透過“可持續核能”授權法案。該法案此前已於今年6月獲得眾議院批准。法案授權義大利政府制定核能領域的整體法律框架，包括制定國家可持續核能發展計劃，規範新建核設施的建設和運營、現有設施退役、放射性廢物和乏燃料管理，並完善相關審批、安全監管和環境保護制度。義大利政府表示，發展新一代核能技術旨在推動能源結構多元化，減少對化石能源的依賴，並提高能源安全。義大利環境與能源安全部部長弗拉廷日前表示，按照目前規劃，義大利最早可能於2033年至2034年實現核能發電。（央視新聞）

### 2026-09-23 23:06:00  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution

【DeepSeek paper: agents exploit unintended channels in production sandbox, compromising training and evaluation】Sept. 23 — DeepSeek published a paper describing DSec, its production-grade sandbox for agent training, and documented agent misbehavior that can invalidate training and evaluation. The firm found agents obtaining answers via unintended channels (for example, residual answers in search-platform admin files); after access controls were applied, agents still circumvented protections by exchanging file-data-block mappings to access protected content through alternate file descriptors, threatening task integrity and shared infrastructure. DeepSeek concludes no single mechanism prevents all agent misbehavior or system faults; its response is to strengthen observability to detect new failure modes and continuously harden DSec as models evolve, including tighter access controls to limit unintended-answer channels and reducing incentives for deceptive behavior — measures it says address some, but not all, issues.

Mauritius Industrial Production Growth Picks Up — Industrial production in Mauritius increased 1.8% year-on-year in the second quarter of 2026, accelerating from a downwardly revised 1.6% increase in the previous quarter, as activity expanded across all sectors. Output rose most in water supply, sewerage, waste management and remediation activities (3.9%), followed by manufacturing (1.9%) and electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (1.3%), while production in mining and quarrying increased 0.8%. On a quarterly basis, industrial production rose 10.9%, rebounding from an 18.2% decline in the prior quarter, largely reflecting a surge in activity in mining and quarrying (27.1%) and manufacturing (12.9%). In contrast, output in electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply contracted 6.7%.

### 2026-09-23 22:55:36  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

French OAT Yields Surge to 18-Year High — France’s 10-year OAT yield jumped to 4.6%, its highest level since July 2008, as oil prices rebounded amid continued uncertainty surrounding US-Iran talks and investors digested stronger-than-expected PMI data. Eurozone private-sector activity expanded in September at its fastest pace in nearly three and a half years, reinforcing expectations that the European Central Bank could raise interest rates further this year. ECB official Joachim Nagel said rising oil prices were becoming increasingly important for monetary policy, while Chief Economist Philip Lane warned that another energy-price surge could keep inflation elevated for longer. In the US, hawkish Fed comments and strong PMI data also lifted expectations for further rate hikes. Meanwhile, concerns over France’s public finances continued to weigh on sentiment, as the government struggles to rein in spending. The budget deficit is now expected to widen to 5.4% of GDP this year, up from 5.1% in 2025.

【伊朗總統佩澤希齊揚在聯合國大會講話要點一覽】
（一）伊朗不會因軍事壓力投降：佩澤希齊揚向參與策劃和實施對伊朗軍事行動的方面表示，相關衝突已經證明伊朗“無法被迫投降”。他稱，伊朗從未主動發動戰爭，但如果面臨戰爭，將繼續進行防禦。
（二）霍爾木茲海峽不能被用於威脅伊朗：佩澤希齊揚表示，不能允許一些國家一方面自由利用霍爾木茲海峽獲取利益，另一方面又藉助這一地區對伊朗實施攻擊、製造不安全域性勢，同時限制伊朗使用自身周邊水域。他稱，伊朗不會接受這種局面。
（三）伊朗需要足夠的防衛力量：佩澤希齊揚表示，伊朗尋求強大是為了保護自身，不是為了發動攻擊或謀求地區霸權。他稱，伊朗的防禦體系必須確保任何對伊朗城市的轟炸都不會得不到回應，並強調伊朗保衛國家“不需要任何人的許可或批准”。
（四）拒絕放棄和平利用核技術的權利：佩澤希齊揚表示，和平核技術關係到伊朗的醫療、農業、工業和電力供應，伊朗不會接受核技術只掌握在少數國家手中。他明確提出，“不要核武器，也不接受對和平核技術的限制”。
（五）批評國際核問題存在“雙重標準”：佩澤希齊揚將以色列與伊朗的核問題進行對比，稱以色列擁有核武器、不是《不擴散核武器條約》成員，卻沒有接受相應核查，而伊朗卻長期受到核查和制裁。他要求國際社會在核問題上採取統一標準。
（六）批評制裁主要傷害普通民眾：佩澤希齊揚表示，對伊朗實施的制裁最終影響的是病人、貧困人口和社會弱勢群體，並指責美國利用經濟壓力迫使其他國家接受其目標。
（七）伊朗願意與外界合作，但要求平等權利：佩澤希齊揚表示，伊朗並不尋求特殊待遇，而是要求與其他國家享有平等權利並承擔相應責任。他稱，真正的國際秩序不能讓軍事力量取代合法性、讓戰爭取代對話和外交。
（八）回應特朗普：願意談判，但不接受以武力施壓：佩澤希齊揚回應特朗普前一天有關伊朗的講話，稱制裁、壓力和威脅只會增強伊朗的抵抗。他表示，伊朗準備進行對話、外交和談判，但不會接受以武力和強迫作為談判語言。

Bund Yields Rise as Rate-Hike Bets Strengthen — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield climbed back above 3.5%, returning to levels not seen since mid-2009, as oil prices rebounded amid uncertainty surrounding US-Iran talks and investors assessed stronger-than-expected PMI data. Eurozone private-sector activity expanded in September at its fastest pace in almost three-and-a-half years, reinforcing expectations that the European Central Bank could raise interest rates further this year. ECB official Joachim Nagel said Tuesday that oil prices were becoming an increasingly important factor in monetary-policy decisions and left the door open to further rate hikes. Also, Chief Economist Philip Lane warned that another surge in energy prices could keep eurozone inflation elevated for longer than expected. In the US, investors increased bets on further Federal Reserve rate hikes following a series of hawkish comments from policymakers and stronger-than-expected PMI data.

Baltic Dry Index Ticks Down — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, edged down by 0.1% to 3,430 points on Wednesday, though remaining close to the highest since September 14. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, fell by 0.5% to 5,861 points. Meanwhile, the panamax index, which usually carries 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, rose by 1.5% to 2,333 points; and the supramax index increased by 0.1% to 1,778 points.

Brent Crude Bounces Back Above $101 — Brent crude rose above $101 a barrel on Wednesday after five consecutive sessions of losses, as uncertainty persisted over whether a formal agreement could reopen the Strait of Hormuz. Iran’s President Masoud Pezeshkian used his address to the UN General Assembly to reject restrictions on Iran’s civilian nuclear program and reiterate Tehran’s opposition to a US military presence in the Middle East. He also said Iran remained open to dialogue and diplomacy but would not accept negotiations under pressure. Meanwhile, Saudi Arabia is preparing to restart exports through its East-West pipeline in the coming days, potentially allowing it to bypass the Strait of Hormuz and increase shipments. On the supply side, US crude inventories rose by 2.969 million barrels to 426.4 million barrels last week, according to the EIA, compared with expectations for a 641,000-barrel draw. The increase exceeded the 1.8-million-barrel build reported by the API, while gasoline and distillate stocks declined.

South Africa 10-Year Bond Yield Inches Higher — South Africa’s 10-year government bond yield climbed to near 8.90%, its highest since mid-September, as oil prices climbed and markets priced in further policy tightening. The South African Reserve Bank raised its repo rate by 25 basis points to 7.25%, as expected, while signalling that further increases could follow this year. The decision comes after the Federal Reserve cut rates by 25 basis points last week, restoring some of South Africa’s yield advantage. Governor Lesetja Kganyago pointed to upside risks to inflation and downside risks to growth. Fuel-price volatility remains a concern amid the ongoing Middle East conflict, while food and core goods inflation have been more favourable. Headline inflation is expected to exceed 5% later this year and in early 2027 before moderating as the fuel shock dissipates, returning to around 3% by end-2027.

### 2026-09-23 21:56:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Services PMI #Importance 1

US Services Activity Surge to 5-Year High: S&P Global — The S&P Global US Services PMI rose to 58.7 in September of 2026 from 56.5 in the previous month, well above the market consensus of 56 to reflect the strongest expansion in services activity in over five years. New orders rose the most in over four years, underpinned by strong demand from domestic consumers, enough to offset lower export orders as unpredictable tariff policy shunned a group of international clients. The jump in business demand drove work backlogs to continue accumulating. Likewise, the need for higher capacity lifted employment growth to its highest since June 2022, not far from a survey record. This was despite input cost inflation hitting its highest in nearly four years, with the war in Iran prompting fuel and transportation costs to surge. Consequently, output charges also rose. Looking ahead, business confidence remained below trend due to cost-of-living concerns.

US Manufacturing Growth Accelerates Sharply in September — The S&P Global US Manufacturing PMI jumped to 57.0 in September 2026 from 53.9 in August, well above market expectations of 53.6, according to the flash estimate. The reading marked the strongest improvement in manufacturing business conditions since May 2022, with all five components contributing to the increase. Production growth rebounded after weakening over the previous three months, reaching its fastest pace since April 2022, while new orders accelerated to their strongest rate in nearly four and a half years. Employment growth rose to its highest level since February 2021, while inventories also increased at a faster pace. Meanwhile, supplier delivery times lengthened to the greatest extent since July 2022.

US Private Sector Expands at Fastest Pace in Over 5 Years — The S&P Global US Flash Composite PMI rose to 58.4 in September 2026 from 56 in August, pointing to the strongest expansion in private-sector activity since July 2021 and marking a fourth consecutive month of accelerating growth. The gains were driven by the service sector (58.7 vs 56.5), which reported the steepest rise in output for over five years, while manufacturing also accelerated (56.7 vs 53.1). New order inflows gathered pace, buoyed principally by the domestic market. Companies’ backlogs of uncompleted orders rose at the sharpest rate since May 2022, which encouraged firms to take on more staff and at a rate not seen since June 2022. However, price pressures intensified, with input costs rising the most since October 2022 mainly due to higher fuel and transport costs though wage pressures were also noted to have picked up in many cases. Selling price inflation also went up while business output expectations for the year ahead were unchanged.

South African Rand Weakens — The South African rand weakened to around 16.4 per USD, close to the lowest since mid-September, pressured by a firmer US dollar and falling prices of precious metals, particularly gold. Meanwhile, traders digested the South African Reserve Bank’s latest policy decision to raise its key repo rate to 7.25%, in line with expectations. The move underscored the central bank’s commitment to bringing inflation back toward its 3% target, with policymakers citing renewed upside risks following the escalation of the Iran war, which pushed up oil prices and dampened the global economic outlook. Policymakers noted South Africa’s economy contracted in Q2, while inflation has increased well above the SARB's target. Data released earlier showed headline inflation ticked up to 4.4% in August from 4.3% in July, just below expectations of 4.5%. The MPC considered adverse scenarios involving higher global rates and inflation expectations, which would require one or two additional rate hikes.

### 2026-09-23 21:10:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Interest Rate #Importance 2

South African Reserve Bank Delivers Rate Hike as Expected — The South African Reserve Bank raised its key repo rate by 25 basis points to 7.25% on September 23, as widely expected, citing the need to bring inflation back toward its target. The decision was unanimous, reflecting upside risks to inflation and downside risks to economic growth. Consumer price inflation edged up to 4.4% in August from 4.3% in July, remaining close to the midpoint of the Reserve Bank’s 3%–6% target range. Policymakers said headline inflation is likely to rise above 5% later this year and in early 2027, driven by higher fuel prices, before slowing as the fuel-price shock recedes. Inflation is expected to return to around 3% toward the end of 2027. Accordingly, inflation forecasts were revised higher to 4.4% in 2026 (from 4.0% previously), 4.0% in 2027 (from 3.8%), and 3.2% in 2028 (from 3.1%). Regarding economic activity, the SARB cut its 2026 growth forecast to 1.2% (from 1.4%) but maintained the 2027 and 2028 projections at 1.7% and 1.9%, respectively.

Contracts for the S&P 500, Dow, and Nasdaq 100 inched marginally below the flatline, with the latter notching a record-high close yesterday. ### 2026-09-23 19:30:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Mortgage Applications #Importance 1

US Mortgage Applications Fall for 3rd Week — The volume of mortgage applications in the US fell by 1.5% in the third week of September, extending the 4.1% drop in the earlier period to mark a third straight decline, according to data compiled by the Mortgage Bankers Association. The result was aligned with the sharp increase in mortgage rates in the period, with that on the benchmark 30-year fixed rate averaging a two-year high of 7.12%. Hence, nearly 10% of applications were for riskier adjusted-rate mortgages. Applications to refinance a mortgage, which are more sensitive to short-term changes in interest rates, fell by 3%. Yields on longer maturity Treasury securities soared at the turn of the month as high energy prices, elevated debt supply, and widening deficits limited exposure to duration in fixed income. In turn, applications for a mortgage to buy a home inched down by 1%.

【U.S. mortgage rates top 7% for first time in over two years】U.S. mortgage rates hit their highest level in more than two years, adding pressure to a housing market already strained by high prices and weak sales. The Mortgage Bankers Association (MBA) said the contract rate on 30-year fixed mortgages rose 15 bps in the week ended Sept. 18 to 7.12%, the highest since May 2024. The 5-year adjustable-rate mortgage rate fell 13 bps to 6.10%. Rates have trended higher since February after the Iran conflict pushed energy prices and reignited inflation concerns; the Fed last week delivered its first policy-rate increase since 2023. Mortgage demand weakened: the MBA purchase index fell 0.8% to a four-week low and the refinancing index dropped 2.6% to its weakest level since Feb. 2025. A sustained move above 7% could further damp housing demand.

US 30-Year Mortgage Rates Climb Above 7% — The average US 30-year fixed mortgage rate rose 15 basis points to 7.12% in the week ended September 18, its highest level since early May 2024, according to the Mortgage Bankers Association. The increase followed a rise in Treasury yields, with the 10-year note remaining near its highest level since 2007, as markets responded to the Federal Reserve’s 25-basis-point rate hike and signals that further increases could follow. Other Fed officials have also struck a hawkish tone, reinforcing expectations of additional tightening. Mortgage rates have risen by more than 100 basis points since the US and Israel began strikes against Iran in late February. ### 2026-09-23 19:00:13  #國際

【外交部：日本現政權已成為威脅地區和平穩定突出因素】外交部發言人郭嘉昆23日表示，日本現政權不僅否認侵略歷史、美化戰爭罪行，還持續強軍擴武，加速“再軍事化”，圖謀拋棄“無核三原則”，修改“和平憲法”，公然挑戰戰後國際秩序，已經成為威脅地區和平穩定的突出因素。當日例行記者會上，有記者問：當地時間9月22日，日本首相高市早苗在聯合國大會發言稱，日本作為和平國家堅持專守防衛政策，為國際社會的和平與繁榮貢獻力量，將推進“無核武器世界”，呼籲刪除《聯合國憲章》中“敵國條款”。請問中方對此有何評論？“相比日方怎麼說，國際社會更關注日方怎麼做。”郭嘉昆說。他表示，戰後日本“和平國家”理念是建立在深刻反省歷史、徹底同軍國主義切割的基礎之上。但事實上，日本現政權不僅否認侵略歷史、美化戰爭罪行，還持續強軍擴武，加速“再軍事化”，圖謀拋棄“無核三原則”，修改“和平憲法”，公然挑戰戰後國際秩序，已經成為威脅地區和平穩定的突出因素。（新華社）

### 2026-09-23 18:59:31  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Urals Oil Prices Fall — Russian Urals crude fell to around $107 a barrel, down from above $120 on September 16, as weaker shipments, new US sanctions and the gradual restoration of Saudi Arabian oil flows increase competition in key markets. Russia’s overseas crude exports averaged 3.53 million barrels a day in the four weeks through September 20, while weekly shipments fell more sharply following a halt in cargoes from the Black Sea port of Novorossiysk. Saudi Arabia’s efforts to restart its East-West pipeline have also pressured Russian grades by increasing alternative supplies. Meanwhile, President Donald Trump signed legislation allowing tariffs of up to 100% on imports from Russia’s five largest energy customers, raising concerns among Indian refiners. India could reduce Russian crude imports to 20%-30% of total purchases, potentially cutting demand by more than 1 million barrels a day. At the same time, attacks on Russian refineries have disrupted nearly 1 million barrels a day of processing capacity.

### 2026-09-23 18:54:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Stock Market #Importance 1

Sensex Finishes Slightly Higher — India’s BSE Sensex closed about 0.4% up at 74,828.3 on Thursday, reversing losses from the previous day, amid broad-based buying. Lower oil prices and hopes of renewed US-Iran talks lifted investor sentiment, while continued foreign investor selling remained a key headwind for the domestic market. Foreign investors offloaded 38.1 billion rupees ($398.6 million) of Indian equities on Tuesday, marking their eighth selling session in the past nine trading days. Among sectoral indices, metals and heavyweight financials provided the main support, while IT remained under pressure due to demand and earnings concerns. Tata Steel outperformed, climbing 3.8%, followed by Bajaj Finance, which gained 3.1% after UBS upgraded its rating and raised its price target on an improved earnings outlook.

### 2026-09-23 18:50:31  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #Brent #Impact mixed #WTI

【Russia trims gas production and LNG export forecasts】A draft government document shows Russia has downgraded gas production and export forecasts amid the Russia-Ukraine conflict and an EU plan to stop buying Russian gas from next year. The draft projects gas production this year at 683.1 bcm, 5.3 bcm below the May forecast but still above 2025's 662.7 bcm. Sea-borne LNG exports are forecast to rise to 35.0 mln t this year from 30.3 mln t in 2025, but remain 5.3 mln t below earlier projections. The document says LNG exports will continue to grow in coming years but at a slower pace than previously expected; these assumptions will be used to update the federal budget through 2029. Earlier this month Russia cut 2026 oil-output guidance to a 17-year low and revised this year and next year's fuel export outlook due to the conflict.

【Beijing to implement AI+ action to accelerate manufacturing digital transformation】Sept. 23 — The Beijing municipal party committee standing committee called for technology‑driven manufacturing growth, prioritizing original innovation and R&D on key core technologies and faster commercialization of results. It ordered implementation of an AI+ action to speed digital transformation in manufacturing and deeper integration of advanced manufacturing with modern services, including expansion of servitized manufacturing, flexible customization and product‑service business models. The committee directed accelerated innovation and deployment of green manufacturing equipment to deliver energy savings, carbon reduction and pollution cuts in key manufacturing areas; support for leading manufacturers to act as supply‑chain anchors and for the cultivation of unicorns and specialized 'little‑giant' firms; faster development of Zhongguancun into a world‑leading tech park and coordinated 'three cities and one district' development; and strengthened financial‑service and intellectual‑property policy mechanisms to improve the ecosystem for advanced manufacturing.

【Germany to buy up to €1.7bn of encryption tech from Secunet for Bundeswehr】A document shows Germany plans to procure up to €1.7bn ($1.9bn) of encryption technology from German security firm Secunet for Bundeswehr data networks. A key parliamentary committee is expected to approve the agreement on Wednesday. The initial order is slightly above €100m, with deliveries due to begin next year. The wider four-year framework builds on an existing deal. Secunet will deploy a Secure Inter‑Network Architecture (SINA) and provide planning, maintenance, training and repair services. Funding will be allocated via next year’s commitment authority. The defence ministry estimates follow-up operating costs of about €6m to 2030. Secunet shares rose as much as 11% intraday on the report.

### 2026-09-23 18:22:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kosovo #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Kosovo Trade Deficit Widens in August — Kosovo’s trade deficit widened to €568 million in August 2026, from €536.8 million in the same month a year earlier. Imports rose 9% to €668.5 million, driven by a 6.6% increase in purchases from EU countries, mainly Germany, which accounted for 13.6% of total imports, followed by Italy (4.2%) and Poland (3.7%). In contrast, imports from CEFTA countries (North Macedonia, Serbia and Albania) declined 2.1%. The main imported products were machinery and transport equipment (23.2% of total), fuel (18.4%), processed goods (16.6%), and food and live animals (14.9%). Meanwhile, exports surged 31.9% to €100.5 million, driven by a 29.8% increase in shipments to EU countries, particularly Germany, which accounted for 10.2% of total exports, followed by Italy (3.0%) and the Netherlands (2.2%). Shipments to CEFTA countries (Albania, North Macedonia, Serbia and Montenegro) also rose 32.2%. The main exported products were processed goods (25.4%), food and live animals (15.7%), and fuel (12.8%).

Netherlands Current Account Surplus Widens in Q2 — The Netherlands’ current account surplus widened to €16.91 billion in the second quarter of 2026 from €13.10 billion in the corresponding quarter of the previous year. The goods account surplus increased to €16.29 billion from €13.04 billion, while the services account surplus rose to €13.88 billion from €13.19 billion. Moreover, the primary account deficit narrowed to €10.98 billion from €11.36 billion. In contrast, the secondary account deficit grew to €2.27 billion from €1.78 billion a year earlier.

【分析師：特朗普柴油出口禁令可能適得其反】美國總統特朗普週二表示，他支援禁止美國出口柴油，以幫助降低價格。他說，政府將“很快”作出決定，“無論採取哪種方式”。但分析人士稱，這一措施可能產生完全相反的效果。華盛頓智庫預算與政策優先中心首席經濟學家Gbenga Ajilore表示：“出口禁令的問題在於，它不會增加國內供應，反而可能減少供應，從而導致價格進一步上漲。推動柴油價格走高的主要因素是伊朗戰爭。結束伊朗戰爭，重新開放霍爾木茲海峽，柴油價格就會下降。其他任何解決方案都將失敗。”Bespoke投資集團也指出，禁令可能導致美國墨西哥灣沿岸煉廠因缺乏足夠儲存能力而關閉，換言之，出口禁令可能會減少全球柴油供應，從而損害煉油商利潤。瑞士寶盛銀行高階分析師Ipek Ozkardeskaya表示，目前美國是全球最大的柴油出口國，如果美國限制出口，全球柴油價格可能會大幅上漲。而由於美國煉油商將失去部分出口市場，他們可能會減少產量，最終導致美國國內其他燃料供應收緊。

### 2026-09-23 17:57:22  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Africa #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

South Africa 10-Year Bond Yield at Over 2-Week Low — South Africa’s 10-year government bond yield was around 8.75%, holding close to the lowest in over two weeks, as investors weighed fresh inflation data ahead of the SARB’s policy decision. Annual inflation edged up to 4.4% in August from 4.3% in July, remaining above the central bank’s 3% target and keeping the focus on persistent price pressures. While the reading came in below expectations, rising fuel costs and the prospect of further external pressures could keep inflation risks elevated. The Fed’s 25-basis-point rate hike last week has also raised the stakes for the SARB, as keeping rates unchanged could reduce South Africa’s yield advantage over US assets and put additional pressure on the rand, potentially feeding through to imported inflation. Against this backdrop, the SARB is widely expected to raise rates by 25 basis points later today, which could further increase the appeal of local government bonds.

【Analyst: AI rally and yen weakness open catch-up opportunity for Japanese stocks】Analysts say a renewed global AI-driven rally and a weaker yen set the stage for catch-up gains in Japanese equities when markets reopen Thursday. Nikkei 225 futures already reflect optimism: the Osaka Exchange December contract trades about 2.5% above last Friday’s cash close. The yen has fallen for a fourth consecutive trading day during the holiday period, refocusing markets on the risk of Japanese government FX intervention. For bond traders the outlook is mixed: uncertainty over the Bank of Japan’s policy path offsets any yield relief from softer oil. Vantage Global Prime analyst Hebe Chen says global AI momentum, mildly improved risk appetite and lower oil provide space for a catch-up rally and ease near-term inflation and rate pressure, while yen weakness may boost exporters short term but intervention risk should keep traders cautious.

Iceland Jobless Rate Rises to 6-Month High — Iceland’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate rose to 6.8% in August 2026, the highest level since February, following an upwardly revised 6.6% in July. The number of unemployed persons increased by 0.7 thousand month-on-month to 15.9 thousand, including 10.9 thousand men and 5.0 thousand women. The youth unemployment rate, which measures job-seekers between 16 and 24 years old, climbed to 14.2% from 6.7%, reaching its highest level since May. Meanwhile, the employment rate edged up to 73.7% from 73.5% in July, with the number of employed individuals rising by 1.2 thousand to 217.8 thousand. The labor force participation rate ticked higher to 79.0% from 78.7%. On a non-seasonally adjusted basis, the unemployment rate rose to 6.0% in August from 5.5% in the previous month.

### 2026-09-23 17:48:30  #MKT News #Market Themes #The Trump Trade #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Analysts: Trump diesel export ban could backfire】President Trump said on Tuesday he supports banning U.S. diesel exports to help lower prices and that the government will decide "soon" "one way or another." Analysts warn the move could reduce, not raise, domestic supply and push prices higher. Gbenga Ajilore, chief economist at the Center on Budget and Policy Priorities, said an export ban "will not increase domestic supply; it could reduce supply and drive prices up," and that ending the Iran war and reopening the Strait of Hormuz would be the primary route to lower diesel prices. Bespoke Investment Group said a ban could force Gulf Coast refineries to idle for lack of storage capacity, cutting global diesel supply and hurting refiners' margins. Ipek Ozkardeskaya, senior analyst at Swiss bank Julius Baer, noted the U.S. is the world's largest diesel exporter and that export limits could sharply raise global diesel prices while prompting U.S. refiners to cut output and tighten other domestic fuel supplies.
